KRONOS MASTER (master-v1-2026-11-01: shared rulebook, roster + schedule layers, learning upgrades, backtests):
  rulebook: R1 The market is the baseline: fair = power de-vig of Pinnacle, blended with other sharp books when 3+ quote both sides at the same line. No model, signal or learned adjustment moves it. | R2 A play is a price, not an opinion: a DraftKings or FanDuel price that beats fair by 2%+, inside 2 hours of start. | R3 No play is the default. No pick for every game, no LEAN, no 0u pick, no quota. | R4 Missing data = unavailable. Never estimate a price, line, status, timestamp or close. Posted lines only. Decimal odds only. Bettable sides only. | R5 Prices must be fresh: sharp quote updated within 10 minutes and not more than 5 minutes older than the soft quote. | R6 Closing line value is the scorecard, and it counts only against a sharp close pulled 10+ minutes after the bet. | R7 Live money: a category (sport x family x kind) is LIVE at 150+ closed bets with valid CLV on the current version and the 95% floor of mean CLV above 0. Otherwise PAPER. No pilot tier, no overrides. | R8 Stake = 25 x (k x EV) / (price - 1) units, rounded to 0.25u; under 0.5u is no play; caps 2u per play, 3u per game, 8u open, 2 plays per game. | R9 Price-edge rows and model rows are never mixed. Models, signals, trends and filters live in the sandbox at zero influence until they pass 150 rows plus a 100-row holdout. | R10 No rule from a named loss: a rule change needs a measured effect over 100+ rows. | R11 A 'full analysis' adds the owner's 15-point checklist as context only; it never creates, sizes or vetoes a play.
